No. 2468/47 – SUNDAY, DECEMBER 28, 2025

(Published by Authority)PART IV (B) - LOCAL GOVERNMENT

Local Government Notifications

Kahawatta Pradeshiya Sabha

Y%S ,xld m%cd;dka;%sl iudcjd§ ckrcfha .eiÜ m;%h

This Gazette Extraordinary can be downloaded from www.documents.gov.lk

1A - PG 7462 — 04 (2025/ 12)

IT is hereby notified for the information of the general public that, in accordance with the duties assigned to the Pradeshiya Sabhas of the Sabaragamuwa Province under the series of Regulations titled “Regulations relating to the preparation of plans and the publication of notices indicating the lists of roads and lanes maintained by Pradeshiya Sabhas in the Sabaragamuwa Province”, published in the Extraordinary Gazette No. 2316/20 dated 24 January 2022 by His Excellency the Governor of the Sabaragamuwa Province, which should be read together with Section 24 of the Pradeshiya Sabha Act, No. 15 of 1987, it has been decided under Secretary’s Decision No. 33 dated 17.06.2025 that the roads and lanes included in the Schedule given below shall be declared as roads and lanes vested in the Kahawatta Pradeshiya Sabha.

Accordingly, this notice is published for the information of the general public. If any road or lane included in the said Schedule passes through any land or forms part of any land, and if the party claiming ownership of such land is of the opinion that the said road or lane belongs to that land, such party is required to institute legal proceedings in the competent court to prove his or her ownership rights to such land before the expiry of a period of three (03) months from the date of this notice.

It is further notified that, if no such legal action is instituted before the expiry of the said period of three months, every road and lane included in the said Schedule shall thereafter be deemed to be a road or lane vested in the Kahawatta Pradeshiya Sabha. G. G. Shantha Priyankara, Chairman, Kahawatta Pradeshiya Sabha.

Dated this 22nd day of December 2025,At the Kahawatta Pradeshiya Sabha Office.

The Gazette of the Democratic Socialist Republic of Sri LankaEXTRAORDINARY
